- **lilypond-pdf-score**: Turn a PDF engraved by LilyPond (Emmentaler music font, as on Mutopia, CPDL and IMSLP) into MusicXML by reading glyph names and positions, when the .ly source is not available and a scan would be poor. One voice per staff (SATB on four staves). Reads notes, rests, dots, flags, beams, ties, accidentals, ficta, clefs, key, time-signature changes, pickups and lyrics, and checks the result against the piece's own MIDI. Use for a LilyPond-made PDF with no source; use lilypond-to-score when the .ly exists and vector-pdf-score for Sibelius PDFs. ([wattdave/ai-score-editing-workflows](https://github.com/wattdave/ai-score-editing-workflows/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lilypond-pdf-score))
- **lilypond-to-score**: Turn a LilyPond (.ly) score, such as a CPDL or IMSLP engraving source, into a readable MusicXML score (for Sibelius, Dorico or MuseScore) with correct bars, clefs and lyrics, via python-ly and this skill's re-barring and lyric scripts. Also covers when a MIDI import is enough, and how to find editable editions of a piece. Use when a piece has LilyPond source but no MusicXML, or when asked to find editable versions of scores. ([wattdave/ai-score-editing-workflows](https://github.com/wattdave/ai-score-editing-workflows/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lilypond-to-score))

- **lindy-effect**: Activate when: user asks 'should we use this old technology or switch to something newer', 'how do I know if a book is worth reading', 'this institution has been around forever — is that meaningful', 'we should modernize / this time is different', 'how long will this practice / tool / framework last'. Do NOT activate when: the item is perishable or has a deterministic life cycle (humans, hardware, organisms); elimination forces are absent and the old thing survives only due to regulatory lock-in.
